As is known, the OTV rate and the minimum victim's tax dollars, envisioned in cigarettes and other tobacco products on the table (III) of the KTV Law (III) list, have been shown one by one in front of the goods on these lists. This ratio and the amount set by the Lawmaker can be increased and reduced by the Council of Ministers within the limits set by the given authority, as it is implemented by the Council of Ministers by the Council of Ministers' Court of Ministers' decision of 63%, with the amount set at 0.1325 euros for one cigarette, and the minimum amount of tax is applied by the Council of Ministers' decision for 0.1450 euros. However, in the Bill and cigarettes, the Council of Ministers has the authority to determine a second victim's tax, which does not exceed 20% of the minimum tax, and to differentiate the amount from the types, properties, packages (packages) and their prices and price groups. Therefore, the executive is given the authority by the TBMM to determine a tax amount that is not predicted, such as the authority of the Lawmaker. This violates the Constitution's 7th amendment, which states that it can't be handed over, and the 73rd amendment to which tax, picture, mortar and so on can only be dealt with by the Law. In addition, the arrangement to raise taxes on alcoholic beverages and other tobacco products, including soda sodas on the III list, will result in a double increase in prices of these products every six months. It will also produce negative results in practice and will cause unnecessary red tape, as the need for the Council of Ministers' decision from time to time to prevent the increase from being implemented. Instead, it is better to apply for the Council of Ministers' decision when it is necessary to increase based on the current verdict. It is recommended that the substance referred to for the specified reasons be removed from the bill. Mr. Dedeoglu, the other motion holder, please.
